\displaystyle+C\left(\|\kappa\|_{0}\right)\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}.

Combining with
D_{1}^{\prime}
and
D_{2}^{\prime},
there holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}+\|\nabla(|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{5}{2}})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{\frac{15}{4}}\|\nabla|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{5}{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\left(\|\kappa\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{20}{11}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{35}{11}}_{L^{5}}

\displaystyle+C\left(\|\kappa\|_{0}\right)\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}.

Integrating with respect to
t
, we get

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla(|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{5}{2}})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\sup_{t}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}ds

\displaystyle+C\left(\|\kappa\|_{0}\right)\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}ds\right)^{\frac{10}{11}}T^{\frac{1}{11}}\sup_{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{35}{11}}_{L^{5}}

\displaystyle+C\left(\|\kappa\|_{0}\right)\sup_{t}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}ds.

By (
2.16
), (
2.17
) and (
2.21
), the proof of (
2.22
) is complete.

ix. the energy norms of
\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}:

\displaystyle\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

(2.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\varepsilon,\tau,\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\chi\|_{0},\|\kappa\|_{0},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|E_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\right)(T+1)^{\frac{41}{4}},

for any
t\in(0,T).

Rewrite the first equation of (
1
) as follow:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\partial_{i}((u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau})=\partial_{i}\Delta n^{\varepsilon,\tau}-\partial_{i}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right),

with
i=1,2,3
. Multiplying it by
\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}
and integrating with respect to space variable
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}((u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle E_{1}+E_{2}.

The term
E_{1}
is same as
A_{1},
we arrive

E_{1}\leq~\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\varepsilon)\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By the product derivative rule for
E_{2},
we have

\displaystyle E_{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{i}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle E_{21}+E_{22}+E_{23}.

For
E_{21},
by
\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}\leq C\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{5}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{5}}
,
Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle E_{21}\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}\|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{5}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{5}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}\|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}.

For
E_{22},
note that
\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}<\tau^{-1},
we obtain

\displaystyle E_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Similarly, for
E_{23}
, we get

\displaystyle E_{23}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}.

Collecting
E_{21}-E_{23},
we have

\displaystyle E_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{16}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}

\displaystyle+~C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}.

Combining
E_{1}
with
E_{2}
, we can yield that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\varepsilon)\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{5}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}.

Noting
\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{6}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{\frac{5}{6}}
, by (
2.21
), (
2.20
), (
2.17
) and (
2.16
),
we can get (
2.23
).

x. the energy norms of
\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}:

\displaystyle\|c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}

(2.24)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\varepsilon,\tau,\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\chi\|_{0},\|\kappa\|_{0},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|E_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\right)(T+1)^{\frac{11}{2}},

for any
t\in(0,T).

Rewrite the second equation of (
1
) as follow:

\partial_{t}\partial_{ij}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\partial_{ij}\left(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)-\partial_{ij}\Delta c^{\varepsilon,\tau}=-\partial_{ij}\left(\frac{\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau}\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)

with
i,j=1,2,3.
Multiplying it by
\partial_{ij}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}
and integrating with respect to space variable
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ij}\left(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)\partial_{ij}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle-~\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ij}\left(\frac{\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau}\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\partial_{ij}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle F_{1}+F_{2}.

The term
F_{1}
is similar as
B_{1},
we arrive

\displaystyle F_{1}\leq~\frac{3}{16}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For the term
F_{2}
, integration by parts yields that

\displaystyle F_{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau}\kappa^{{}^{\prime}}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{i}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{ijj}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{ijj}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\kappa\|_{0})\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\kappa\|_{0})\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{2}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{2}.

Combining
F_{1}
with
F_{2}
, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

\displaystyle+~C(\|\kappa\|_{0})\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\kappa\|_{0})\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{2}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{2}.

By (
2.17
), (
2.15
), (
2.20
), (
2.21
), and (
2.16
), we have (
2.24
).

xi. the energy norms of
|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}:

\displaystyle\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{3}_{x}}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(2.25)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\varepsilon,\tau,\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\chi\|_{0},\|\kappa\|_{0},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|E_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\right)(T+1)^{\frac{321}{8}},

for any
t\in(0,T).

Rewrite the first equation of (
1
) as follow:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\partial_{i}((u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau})=\partial_{i}\Delta n^{\varepsilon,\tau}-\partial_{i}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right),

with
i=1,2,3
. Multiplying it by
|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}
and integrating with respect to space variable
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{3}+\frac{8}{9}\|\nabla(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}((u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle S_{1}+S_{2}.

For the term
S_{1}
, Hölder inequality and interpolation inequality yield that

S_{1}\leq~\|\partial_{i}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{3}\leq~C(\varepsilon)\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}.

By the product derivative rule for
S_{2},
there holds

\displaystyle S_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\right)\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\left(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{i}\left(\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle S_{21}+S_{22}+S_{23}.

For
S_{21}
,
due to

\displaystyle\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{9}{2}}}\leq\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{\frac{2}{9}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{\frac{7}{9}},

there holds

\displaystyle S_{21}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\left|\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\right|^{2}dx+C\|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{9}{2}}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\right|^{2}dx+C\|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{\frac{7}{3}}.

Similarly, for
S_{22}
and
S_{23},
we have

\displaystyle S_{22}+S_{23}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|\nabla\left(|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)\right|^{2}dx+C(\tau)\int\left(|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}+|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{4}\right)|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|.

Then we obtain

\displaystyle\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{3}_{x}}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\varepsilon)\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle+C\|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{\frac{7}{3}}ds

\displaystyle+C(\tau)\int_{0}^{t}\int\left(|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}+|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{4}\right)|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|dxds.

By (
2.17
) and (
2.23
), we know

\displaystyle\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}ds\leq CT^{\frac{129}{8}}.

By
(
2.24
), (
2.23
) and the interpolation inequality

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{q}L^{p}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|f\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right),\quad\frac{2}{q}+\frac{3}{p}=\frac{3}{2},\quad 2\leq p\leq 6,

(2.26)

there holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{\frac{7}{3}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t,x}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}_{t,x}}^{\frac{7}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{3}_{x}}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\sup_{t}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{9}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t,x}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{3}_{x}}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+CT^{\frac{99}{4}+\frac{123}{8}}.

By
(
2.24
), (
2.23
) and the interpolation inequality

\displaystyle C(\tau)\int_{0}^{t}\int\left(|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}+|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{4}\right)|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\tau)\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t,x}}^{2}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{5}{2}}_{t,x}}+\sup_{t}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L_{t}^{1}L^{3}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CT^{\frac{11}{2}+\frac{43}{8}}+CT^{11+\frac{47}{8}}.

Thus the proof of (
2.25
) is complete.

xii. the energy norms of
\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}:

\displaystyle\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}

(2.27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\varepsilon,\tau,\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\chi\|_{0},\|\kappa\|_{0},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|E_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}})(T+1)^{54},

for any
t\in(0,T).

We write the second equation of
(
1
)
as follow:

\partial_{t}\partial_{ijk}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\partial_{ijk}\left(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)-\partial_{ijk}\Delta c^{\varepsilon,\tau}=-\partial_{ijk}\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right),

with
i,j,k=1,2,3.

Multiplying above equation by
\partial_{ijk}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}
and integrating with respect to space variable
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ijk}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{ijk}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ijk}\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\partial_{ijk}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle H_{1}+H_{2}.

For
H_{1}
, by Hölder inequality, we have

\displaystyle H_{1}\leq

\displaystyle C\big[\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+~\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{2}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+~\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}).

Next, we deal with
H_{2}.
Similarly, we have

\displaystyle H_{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\tau,\|\kappa\|_{0})\big[\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+~\left(\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right)+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{4}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{4}}\big].

Collecting
H_{1}
and
H_{2},
we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{4}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{2}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle+C(\tau,\|\kappa\|_{0})\big[\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+~\left(\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right)+\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{4}}\big].

(
2.18
), (
2.19
) and (
2.22
) show that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{2}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{5}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}}^{2}ds+C\sup_{t}\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\sup_{t}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle+~C\sup_{t}\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{t}\|\nabla^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CT^{8}.

(
2.23
), (
2.20
) and (
2.26
) imply

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{4}}+\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{4}_{L^{4}}ds\leq CT^{54}.

Moreover, (
2.23
), (
2.20
) and (
2.24
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t,x}}T^{\frac{2}{5}}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{2}{25}}_{L^{1}}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{48}{25}}_{L^{2}}+CT\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{6}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CT^{\frac{787}{50}}+CT^{\frac{55}{4}}.

The proof of (
2.27
) is complete.

xiii. the energy norms of
\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}:

\displaystyle\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}

(2.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\varepsilon,\tau,\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\chi\|_{0},\|\kappa\|_{0},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|E_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}}\right)(T+1)^{\frac{411}{8}},

for any
t\in(0,T).

Rewrite the first equation of (
1
) as follow:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\partial_{ij}((u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau})=\partial_{ij}\Delta n^{\varepsilon,\tau}-\partial_{ij}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right),

with
i,j=1,2,3
. Multiplying it by
\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}
and integrating with respect to space variable
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ij}((u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ij}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle G_{1}+G_{2}.

The term
G_{1}
is same as
B_{1},
we arrive

\displaystyle G_{1}\leq\frac{3}{16}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\varepsilon)\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\varepsilon)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By the product derivative rule for
G_{2}
, we also have

\displaystyle G_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ij}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{ij}(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}})(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{i}(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}})\partial_{j}(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j}(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}})\partial_{i}(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{ij}(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle G_{21}+G_{22}+G_{23}+G_{24}.

For
G_{21},
note that

\displaystyle\partial_{ij}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\right)

\displaystyle=~\partial_{i}\left(\frac{\partial_{j}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=~\frac{\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})-2\tau\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{j}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{3}},

we have

\displaystyle G_{21}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle-~\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{2\tau\partial_{i}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{j}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{3}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+~C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+~C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}.

For
G_{22},
note that

\displaystyle\partial_{j}\left(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)=\partial_{j}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})+\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{j}c^{\varepsilon,\tau},

we obtain

\displaystyle G_{22}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\partial_{j}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\partial_{j}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle+~\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\partial_{j}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{j}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle G_{221}+G_{222}.

For
G_{221},

\displaystyle G_{221}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

For
G_{222},

\displaystyle G_{222}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Then

\displaystyle G_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

The estimation of term
G_{23}
is same as
G_{22},
we omit it.
For
G_{24},
note that

\displaystyle\partial_{ij}\left(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\left(\partial_{j}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})+\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{j}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{ij}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})+\partial_{j}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{i}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\partial_{i}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{j}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle+~\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{i}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{j}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{ij}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\left(|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|+|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}||\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|+|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{3}\right),

we get

\displaystyle G_{24}\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{ij}(\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\left(|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|+|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}||\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|+|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{3}\right)\nabla\partial_{ij}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{6}\right).

Collecting
G_{21}-G_{24},
we have

\displaystyle G_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{6}\right).

Then combining
G_{1}
with
G_{2},
we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{11}{16}\|\nabla^{3}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\varepsilon)\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\varepsilon)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla^{2}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+C\left(\tau,\|\chi\|_{0}\right)\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{6}}^{6}\right).

Due to

\displaystyle\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}},

there holds

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\|\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq CT^{\frac{329}{16}}+C.

(2.29)

Integrating with respect to time variable
t
from 0 to
T
,
by (
2.17
), (
2.23
), (
2.25
), (
2.27
) and (
2.29
), we can get (
2.28
).
To sum up the above estimates, for any
t\in(0,T),
we achieve (
2.14
).

Then we complete the proof of Theorem
1.4
.

3.
Uniform estimates for the regularized system

For the problem (
1
), one can obtain the following uniform estimates independent of
\varepsilon
and
\tau
.

Proposition 3.7
.

Let
(n_{0}^{\varepsilon},c_{0}^{\varepsilon},u_{0}^{\varepsilon})
satisfying

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\displaystyle n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\in L^{1},\quad(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\ln(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\in L^{1},\quad u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}_{\sigma};\\
\displaystyle\nabla\sqrt{c_{0}^{\varepsilon}}\in L^{2},\quad c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\in L^{1}\cap L^{\infty};\\
\displaystyle n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\geq 0,\quad c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\geq 0.\\
\end{array}\right.

Assume that
(n^{\varepsilon,\tau},c^{\varepsilon,\tau},u^{\varepsilon,\tau})
is a solution of (
1
) as in Theorem
1.4
,
\kappa
and
\chi
satisfy
(
1.11
)
,
(
1.12
)
and
\eqref{equ:chi kappa}.

Then there exists a constant
C>0
independent of
\varepsilon
and
\tau
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{U^{\varepsilon,\tau}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{V^{\varepsilon,\tau}}(\tau)d\tau

(3.5)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{1}},\|u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}},\|(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\ln(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\|_{L^{1}},\|\nabla\sqrt{c_{0}^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}})(1+t),

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{U^{\varepsilon,\tau}}(t)=||n^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{1}}+\|(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\|_{L^{1}}+\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.6)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{V^{\varepsilon,\tau}}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle||\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}||\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{2}}^{2}

(3.7)

\displaystyle+~\frac{1}{3\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}.

Proof.

First, we rewrite the equation of
n^{\varepsilon,\tau}
in
(\ref{eq:CNS})_{1}
as

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)+(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)-\Delta(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)

\displaystyle=-\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)+\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right).

(3.8)

Multiplying
(1+\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1))
in
(\ref{n'})
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(1+\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1))+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(1+\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1))

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Delta(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(1+\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1))+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)(1+\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1))

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)(1+\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1))=0.

Integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(\cdot,t)dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)|^{2}

(3.9)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1).

For the equation of
c^{\varepsilon,\tau}
in (
1
), noting that

\displaystyle\Delta c^{\varepsilon,\tau}=2|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}+2\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}},

and dividing
2\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}
on both sides, we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}+u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}-\frac{|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}}{\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}-\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}=-\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{2\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}).

(3.10)

Multiplying the above equation (
3.10
) by
-\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})
, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla^{2}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}dx

(3.11)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}dx

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

The key is to estimate the term
I_{1}
. By integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle I_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\partial_{ii}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i,j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}+2\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle+~2\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}.

Noting that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle=-I_{1}-\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ii}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}},

we have

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i,j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ii}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}.

Using Young inequality it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}|\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2},

and

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ii}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}+\frac{4}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}.

Then we have

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}+\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}.

Using
\kappa(0)=0
and mean value theorem, for some
\eta\in(0,1)
, there holds

\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})=c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})-\kappa(0)}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}=c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\kappa^{\prime}(\eta c^{\varepsilon,\tau}).

Noting that
\kappa^{\prime\prime}(s)\geq 0
and
\kappa^{\prime}(s)\geq 0
from
(
1.12
)
, there means
\kappa^{\prime}(0)\geq 0
and

\kappa^{\prime}(\eta c^{\varepsilon})\leq 2\kappa^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon}),

and we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla\left(\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\right)~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

(3.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa^{\prime}(\eta c^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 0.

Then for
I_{3}
, integration by parts yields that

\displaystyle I_{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

(3.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\nabla(\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\nabla(\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla(\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}))\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}.

Collecting
(
3.11
)
,
(
3
)
and
(
3.14
)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla^{2}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}.

Since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{ii}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}=||\nabla^{2}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2},

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{3}||\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}.

(3.15)

Then
(\ref{ine:energy estimate of n})+\frac{4}{\Theta_{0}}\times(\ref{ine:energy estimate of c})
yields that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(\cdot,t)dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)|^{2}

(3.16)

\displaystyle+~\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}||\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+~\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)dx

\displaystyle+~\frac{4}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}:=J_{1}+J_{2}.

Estimate of
J_{1}.

Noting that
\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\leq 1,
and
\|\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|\chi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}})}\leq C,

by integration by parts, Hölder inequality, Young inequality, (
2.10
) and (
2.12
), there holds

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)dx

(3.17)

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{\frac{3}{2}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\frac{\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}dx

\displaystyle\leq C\|\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{4}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|\frac{\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{1}}\|c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{\infty}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|\frac{\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{(1+n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{2}}\right|^{2}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{27\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}dx

\displaystyle~~+C(\Theta_{0},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}).

Estimate of
J_{2}.

For
J_{2}
, we derive

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{4}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}-\frac{4}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

(3.18)

\displaystyle=-\frac{4}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{j}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{27\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}dx+\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}||c^{\varepsilon}_{0}||_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}dx.

Noting that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}|^{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\sum_{i=j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}})^{2},

combining
(
3.17
)
and
(
3.18
)
, we achieve

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(\cdot,t)dx+3\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}|^{2}

\displaystyle+~\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}||\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{3\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq~\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}||c^{\varepsilon}_{0}||_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}+C(\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}).

(3.19)

Finally, for the equation of
u^{\varepsilon,\tau}
, multiplying the equation
(\ref{eq:CNS})_{3}
by
u^{\varepsilon,\tau}
and integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}((n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi)\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot u^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx.

Due to

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}((n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi)\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot u^{\varepsilon,\tau}dx=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi\cdot(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})dx,

it follows that

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi\cdot(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})dx.

(3.20)

Note that

\displaystyle\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{3}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left((\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1)^{2}+2\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-2\right)^{3}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1)^{6}+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1)^{3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}|^{2}\right)^{3}+C\int_{\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\leq 1\}}(\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1)^{3}

\displaystyle+~C\int_{\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}>1\}}(\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1)^{3}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle C\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}|^{2}\right)^{3}+K_{1}+K_{2}.

For
K_{1},
due to
\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1\leq 2n^{\varepsilon,\tau}
for
n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\leq 1,
there holds

\displaystyle K_{1}

\displaystyle\leq~C\int_{\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\leq 1\}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau})^{3}\leq C\int_{\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\leq 1\}}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\leq C\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}.

For
K_{2},
clearly,
\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1\leq\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}
and
\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\leq 4(\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1)
for
n^{\varepsilon,\tau}>1,
then we have

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle\leq~C\int_{\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}>1\}}(\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1})^{3}

\displaystyle\leq~C\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}>1\}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{6}\{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}>1\}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~C\|\sqrt{2n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}-1\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~C\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Combining
K_{1}
and
K_{2},
it follows that

\displaystyle\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{3}\leq C\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{6}+C\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}.

(3.21)

Using (
3.21
), the right term of
(\ref{u})
can be estimated as follow:

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi\cdot(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle||\nabla\phi||_{L^{\infty}}||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{6}}||n^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{6}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8(\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+1)}\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{3}+\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{16}{7}}\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{13}{7}}+\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{5}{3}}\right).

Then from (
3.20
) we can obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}

(3.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8(\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+1)}\|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}}),

where we use
||\nabla\phi||_{L^{\infty}}\leq C
and

||n^{\varepsilon}_{0}||_{L^{1}}\leq C.

Therefore, (
3.22
)
\times 2(\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}||c^{\varepsilon}_{0}||_{L^{\infty}}+1)
+(
3
) yields that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+2(\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}||c^{\varepsilon}_{0}||_{L^{\infty}}+1)||\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(\cdot,t)dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}||\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{3\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{27}{2\Theta_{0}}||c^{\varepsilon}_{0}||_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}+C\left(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|n^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}\right),

which means

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}||u^{\varepsilon,\tau}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+||\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)||_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)(\cdot,t)dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\frac{d}{dt}||\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}||\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}(t)}||_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{3\Theta_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}}).

Integrating with respect to time from 0 to
t
, we get (
3.5
).

Proof of (
1.18
) when
\mu=0
.

Rewrite the equation of
u^{\varepsilon,\tau}
as follows:

\partial_{t}(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}-\Delta(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}+\nabla(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}P^{\varepsilon,\tau}=-(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi)\ast\rho^{\varepsilon}.

Multiplying
(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}
in above equation, integration by parts yields that

\displaystyle\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{t}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}ds+\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}^{2}\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{2}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}+\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}^{2}\|\nabla(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}^{4}+\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which follows that

\displaystyle\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(3.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}^{4}+\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Note that

\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

by (
3.21
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{3}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}ds+\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}+1}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}ds+\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{3}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}},\|(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\ln(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\|_{L^{1}},\|\nabla\sqrt{c_{0}^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}})(t+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}^{4}\leq C(t+1).

(3.24)

By (
3.24
),
\nabla\phi\in L^{\infty}
and the Sobolev inequality (also see Lemma
8.19
)

\|f\|_{L^{q}}\leq C\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}f\|_{L^{p}},\quad{\rm for}\quad\frac{1}{q}=\frac{1}{p}-\frac{\alpha}{n},

(
3.23
) becomes

\displaystyle\|(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla(-\Delta)^{\frac{1}{4}}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}},\|n_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|c_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}},\|u_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}},\|(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\ln(n_{0}^{\varepsilon}+1)\|_{L^{1}},\|\nabla\sqrt{c_{0}^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}})(t+1).

4.
Local energy inequality

Lemma 4.8

(local energy inequality)
.

Let
\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
is an arbitrary bounded domain and
\psi
is a cut-off function, which vanishes on the parabolic boundary of
\Omega\times(0,t)
.
Assume
(n^{\varepsilon,\tau},c^{\varepsilon,\tau},u^{\varepsilon,\tau})
is a regular solution of (
1
) in
\Omega\times(0,t)\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}
with initial data

(
3.7
)
. Assume that the function
\kappa
and
\chi
satisfy
(
1.11
)
,
(
1.12
)
and
\eqref{equ:chi kappa}.
Then the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln(n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\psi)(\cdot,t)+4\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi+\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{\Omega}(|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi)(\cdot,t)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{3\Theta_{0}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi+\frac{2}{3\Theta_{0}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\Theta_{0}}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\Omega}(|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2})(\cdot,t)\psi+\frac{18}{\Theta_{0}}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi)+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi)+\frac{2}{\Theta_{0}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}{(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})}\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\Theta_{0}}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\left(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi\right)+\frac{18\mu}{\Theta_{0}}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{36}{\Theta_{0}}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(P^{\varepsilon,\tau}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\tau})u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi-\frac{36}{\Theta_{0}}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla\phi)\ast\rho^{\varepsilon}\cdot u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi.

(4.1)

Proof.

Multiplying
(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\psi
in
(\ref{eq:CNS})_{1}
, integration by parts yields that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\psi+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\psi

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\Delta n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\psi+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\nabla\cdot\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\psi

\displaystyle\doteq T_{1}+\cdots+T_{4}=0,

where

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{t}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}{(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})}\psi dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}{\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)(\cdot,t)dx-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{t}\psi dxdt;

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi dxdt-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\psi dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\psi dxdt;

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\frac{1}{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi dxdt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi dxdt-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\Delta\psi dxdt;

and

\displaystyle T_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)(1+\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\psi dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi dxdt.

To sum up, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(n^{\varepsilon,\tau}{\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)(\cdot,t)+4\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

(4.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi)+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}(u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\ast\rho^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\ln n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\left(\frac{n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\left(\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\chi(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})\right)\cdot\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi.

Note that

\displaystyle\Delta{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}=2|\nabla\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}|^{2}+2\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\Delta\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}},

and dividing
2\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}
on both sides, it follows from the equation
(\ref{eq:CNS})_{2}
that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}+u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}-\frac{|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}}{\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}-\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}=-\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{2\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}).

(4.3)

Multiplying the above equation
(\ref{eq:CNS})_{2}
by
-\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\psi)
and integration by parts, there holds

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{t}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)dxdt-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}}{\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)dxdt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{2\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\partial_{i}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)dxdt

\displaystyle\doteq J_{1}+J_{2}+\cdots+J_{5}=0,

where

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{t}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\psi=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}(|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi)(\cdot,t)-\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\partial_{t}\psi;

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{i}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}:(\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\otimes\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})\psi-\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}};

\displaystyle J_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{i}\big((\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\big)(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\big((\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\big)\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\big((\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\big)\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\cdot\nabla\psi;

\displaystyle J_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{ij}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi)=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\partial_{j}\psi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi-\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\Delta\psi;

and

\displaystyle J_{5}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla\left(\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{2\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\right)~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\tau}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi.

Combining all of them, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}(|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi)(\cdot,t)+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi)+\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}:(\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\otimes\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})\psi-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\cdot\nabla\psi-\frac{1}{\tau}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa^{\prime}(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\tau}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi.

(4.4)

We remark that the bad terms are those integrals without
\nabla\psi
.
One bad term of all the above terms is
I\doteq-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi
. The estimation of
I
is similar to
I_{1}
from (
3.11
) (see also (2.6) in
[
5
]
).
Then we have

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i=j}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\psi+\sum_{i\neq j}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\partial_{ij}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

(4.5)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-1}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\cdot\nabla\psi.

Submitting it to (
4
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}(|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi)(\cdot,t)+\frac{1}{3}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\triangle\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla\left(\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{2\tau\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}}\right)~~\ln(1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau})\cdot\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\psi

(4.6)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3}\sum_{i=j}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\psi

\displaystyle\leq~\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi)+\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}u^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla\psi

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}:(\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\otimes\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})\psi-\frac{1}{4}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\frac{\kappa(c^{\varepsilon,\tau})}{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\frac{1}{1+\tau n^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\nabla n^{\varepsilon,\tau}\cdot\nabla c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\psi.

Note that
\|c^{\varepsilon,\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|c^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}
and

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}\psi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\psi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\sum_{i=j}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{{c}^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\psi,

using Young’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}:(\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}\otimes\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})\psi

(4.7)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{18}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}\psi+\frac{9}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{18}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{4}\psi+\frac{9}{2}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\psi

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{6}\sum_{i=j}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}(\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{-2}(\partial_{j}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}(\partial_{i}\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}})^{2}\psi+\frac{9}{2}\|{c^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\tau}|^{2}\psi.

Substitute these estimates (
4.5
)-(
4.7
) to the inequality of (
4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}(|\nabla\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi)(\cdot,t)+\frac{1}{3}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega}|\Delta\sqrt{c^{\varepsilon,\tau}}|^{2}\psi

(4.8)
